HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B001","root_003249/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_09cbee0d6676519b4790","text":"2. **Still prayer, still conscience.** Evidence: `س ه و B002` stillness; activated by `س ك ن B001` cessation of motion and `ح ض ض B001` urging. Structure: the actor does not urge; his prayer is “still” where it should move him and others. Mechanism: سهو becomes inertness: a calm, unmoved religious posture before hunger. Abductive move: I supply the moral reading of stillness as failure to be moved; the packet supplies stillness and urging. Reading change: ساهون can carry paralysis, not only distraction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_003249/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0b9c948cfe754c78b511","text":"107:6-107:7 together produced the strongest later surprise. Public visibility activated the front rack of `س ه و/B004`, while the withheld, household-scale `الماعون` activated both the goods placed there and the easily missed smallness of `س ه و/B005`; this corrected “heedlessness” from a generic cognitive lapse to an attention system that stages prayer and filters out unshowy duty.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B005","root_003249/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_44bf646827b666b651a1","text":"4. **A front-stage visibility system makes subtle duties disappear.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `س ه و/B004` is a porch or front rack on which goods are placed, and fixed `س ه و/B005` is the tiny, hidden star missed by sight; 107:6's `ر ء ي/B005` is showing off, while 107:7's `م ع ن/B005` includes household goods and small available benefits. **Structural cue:** `ساهون` is followed first by public display and then by withheld household-scale aid. **Assigned role:** the front rack models staged visibility, while the hidden star models the low-visibility obligation attention fails to register. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the material and optical images; I infer one attentional economy in which prayer is placed on the visible frontage and unshowy assistance is screened out. **Reading change:** being “heedless of their prayer” includes converting it into display while missing the small practical duties that would realize it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_003249/B002","root_003249/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_759439b15f02cadcc8a0","text":"107:6 made the hidden-star branch (`س ه و B005`) more forceful than expected: the ostentatious person sees the audience and misses the small duty. 107:7 also made stillness (`س ه و B002`) social: unmoved prayer culminates in the unmoved hand that withholds.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_003249/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8e017e9c53afc79ee18d","text":"3. **Overlooking fault versus refusing to overlook need.** Evidence: `س ه و B003` easy dealing by overlooking another's slip; activated by harsh `د ع ع B001` toward the orphan. Structure: a branch of social gentleness is absent where the vulnerable appears. Mechanism: the person “overlooks” prayer’s claim while not gently overlooking the orphan’s weakness. Abductive move: I supply the reversal between indulgence toward self and harshness toward dependent; the packet supplies easy-dealing and rough-driving branches. Reading change: the ayah exposes a crooked distribution of leniency.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_003249/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_af604f0cba47b543e5dd","text":"4. **The hidden star of missed obligation.** Evidence: `س ه و B005` the tiny star missed by sight; activated by `ر ء ي B001/B013` seeing/tell-me and `ر ء ي B005` showiness. Structure: seeing is thematized at 107:1 and 107:6, while this ayah names what is missed. Mechanism: showy vision amplifies what is obvious and misses the small, dim obligation. Abductive move: I infer that small duties are like the hidden star; the packet supplies visual and hidden/missed branches. Reading change: prayer-heedlessness becomes failure to perceive quiet claims such as orphan, miskīn, and maʿūn.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_003249/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b165786afec9b146d008","text":"1. **Heedlessness as turned-away heart.** Evidence: `س ه و B001` heedlessness of heart, including in prayer, with `ص ل و B003` prescribed worship. Structure: the preposition `عن` marks being away from their ṣalāh, not simply within it. Mechanism: the rite exists, but attention has departed from its meaning and obligations. Abductive move: I infer that “away from” spatializes the heart’s departure; the packet supplies prayer and heedlessness. Reading change: the ayah reads as alienation from prayer rather than accidental forgetfulness alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_003249/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cbb96181fc99abfa1a4a","text":"3. **Leniency is applied inward and denied outward.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `س ه و/B003` is easy dealing through overlooking another's slip; 107:2's `د ع ع/B001` is harsh rebuffing, and fixed `صلاتهم` marks the prayer as “their” own. **Structural cue:** the group overlooks the failure attached to its own prayer while its conduct toward the orphan is forceful rather than easy or forgiving. **Assigned role:** the easy-dealing branch exposes an asymmetry in where forbearance is spent. **Abductive move:** the packet does not explicitly say they excuse themselves; I infer self-exemption from the possessive prayer, adverse qualification, and contrasting harshness. **Reading change:** heedlessness becomes selective moral indulgence: their own ritual failure is passed over, another's vulnerability is not.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B003","root_003249/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cc0dbcae3307eef283e1","text":"2. **The prayer has movement but no ethical output.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `س ه و/B002` is stillness; fixed `ص ل و/B003` includes a prescribed act with standing, bowing, and prostration, while nearby `س ك ن/B001` is cessation of movement and 107:7's `م ن ع/B002` is a barrier. **Structural cue:** bodily worship is followed by showmanship and a blocked benefit, after earlier failure to mobilize food. **Assigned role:** the stillness branch marks arrested consequence rather than absent bodily motion. **Abductive move:** the packet does not state that prayer must cause aid; I infer that a properly orienting ritual should move the agent toward the obligations already placed beside it. **Reading change:** `ساهون` describes ethically inert prayer: much performed motion, but no movement toward the vulnerable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e78d2f626d354cf560b2","text":"1. **Heedlessness is an orientation away, not merely a lapse inside prayer.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ص ل و/B003` is prescribed worship, and fixed `س ه و/B001` is heedlessness in which the heart turns away from a thing, including forgetfulness in prayer. **Structural cue:** the construction uses `عن صلاتهم`; 107:6-107:7 then show stable audience-seeking and withholding rather than a momentary ritual mistake. **Assigned role:** `سهو` supplies the absent attention, while `عن` gives that absence a direction. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the grammar and repeated conduct; I infer disposition from their recurrence. **Reading change:** the ayah diagnoses a life turned away from what prayer requires, not simply accidental inattention during one performance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
